D 1 - XII, Mazmour. With 1 AI. No. 15 [A] Serve the Lord with fear and exult for Him with trembling. For eternal is His mercy. From generation to generation [extends] His justice. We who are His people, enter before Him rendering humble thanks, and in His courts celebrate Him with canticles. For [eternal is His mercy]. Let us fast and love our neighbor and love one another. For. Honor the Sabbath and cultivate justice, for the Sabbath was created for man. For [eternal is His mercy]. I will sing to You of mercy and judgment. D 8 - XLII, 'Ezl of the morning. [Serayou (T AI. No. 22)]. No. 29 [E] Saul was approaching the city of Damascus; suddenly a lightning flash shone above him, from the height of heaven. And He said to him: "Saul, my brother, I have been sent to you, in order to explain and announce to you that I have made you a chosen instrument so that you may carry my name to the nations." Tell him: he said to me: "The very one who formerly persecuted [the Church] now teaches the faith." As [the Lord] says in the Gospel: You will announce to other cities [the good News], He announced it to Paul saying: "Let us exercise ourselves in justice. Let us not go into trickery and let us not falsify the word of the Lord. Let us honor the Sabbath according to justice."

h) With 4 AI. no. 75 [G] Let us honor the Sabbath and let us not abandon the institution of our Savior Jesus Christ, for He [!] commanded Moses, His servant, and Abraham, His beloved, and Adam, His first work, so that He might have mercy on us and send us His clemency, when He places the sheep on His right and the goats on His left. D 8A - XXXIVA-XXXVI A, 'Aboun-[Mazmour]. With 1 AI. no. 112 [G] Splendor and light is the Church. Whoever rises early in the morning and goes toward it will not experience sorrow. Splendor and light is the Church. He gave it to us [as] the gate of life. Watch in it, pray and ask that you do not enter into temptation. Splendor. In the time of Noah, it was the salvation, the one that became the gate of life. Splendor. It is visible from afar like a lamp in the midst of darkness. Splendor. Like the lily flower and like the pomegranate that are in the garden. Splendor. With the power of the heavens when the King of glory shall come. Splendor. They will not abandon it, the City that they desire. Splendor and light is the Church. Splendor and light is the Church.

D 2--III-V, 'Aboun-[Mazmour]. no. 121 [GA] Moses said in the Law: It is a good land and not like the land of Egypt. A good land that the Lord watches over constantly from [the beginning of] the year until the beginning of the cycle of the [following] year. A good land and not like the land of Egypt. Jacob said: "I saw a ladder from heaven. There shall be built the house of the Lord. He built his sanctuary in the highest of the heavens. David said: "This is the place of my rest forever. There, I will dwell, for I have chosen it. He built [his sanctuary]. The wing of the dove is made of silver and its sides have the greenish reflection of gold. The walls of the Church are adorned with sapphires and topazes. They are adorned with rubies and emeralds. They are adorned by the names of the 15 prophets. They are adorned by the names of the 12 apostles. A rampart and roses surround it. A man is born in it: the King of Israel, whose reign shall have no end. There [is] the dwelling of the Most High. The King of Israel, the Savior of the world, the Lord dwells upon it, the city that He finds pleasing.

No. 428 [A] You established the foundations of the earth so that it would not waver. You stretched out the heaven like a vault. You modeled our flesh in your own likeness. And having accomplished everything, He rested on the [day of the] Sabbath. Moses commanded the people concerning the Sabbath: "Abstain from all servile work on that [day]." The Lord said: "As for you, take care [of your souls]. Purify your bodies. Store up for yourselves a treasure in the heavens. The affairs of this world: gold and silver, honors and riches are fleeting. As for you, take care [of your souls]. Purify your bodies. Store up for yourselves a treasure in the heavens, for every man whose treasure is pure [is] the hidden dwelling of the Holy Spirit.

No. 431 [GAG] My brothers, remember the day of your death and think that it will come for you: for the day of Christ approaches and his sovereign reign arrives. Honors and riches are fleeting. Sumptuous garments wear out. Honor the Sabbath according to justice. My brothers, give, lend. Store up for yourselves a treasure in the heavens, where worms and moths do not destroy it and the thief does not find it.

e) No. 1144 [A] They built the Church and established its foundations upon the Holy Spirit, so that its appearance might be heavenly, that it might save us from the furnace of fire, that it might give us joy, justice, and peace, and in addition to peace, He who can do all things and to whom nothing is impossible.

c) With 4 Al. no. 1150 [G] The Cross is a defense and a rampart, the foundations of the Church. This Cross is the calm of the sea and the peace on earth.
